# Sound event references in game data

Generated by `scripts/sound_refs.py` from the unpacked `aces`, `char` and `game` vromfs
and `docs/EVENTS.md`. Each row: event, the data fields that name it, where (file count per field).
Events with no row are started from native code by path or GUID (crew dialogs, chat, music cues).
